°C\nPREHEAT_END_TEMP = 150.0    # °C\n\n# Maximum preheat ramp rate (Page 5)\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\n\n# Time Above Liquidus (TAL) requirements (Page 5)\nMIN_TAL = 30.0  # seconds\nMAX_TAL = 60.0  # seconds\n\n# Peak temperature requirement (Page 5)\nPEAK_TEMP_OFFSET = 20.0  # °C above liquidus\n\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"  MES log: {len(mes_log)} runs\")\n    print(f\"  Thermocouple data: {len(thermocouples)} records\")\n    print(f\"  Defect data: {len(defects)} records\")\n\n    return mes_log, thermocouples, defects\n\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp_rate(tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> Tuple[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat zone (100-150°C) for a single TC.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (tc_id, max_ramp_rate_c_per_s)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter data in preheat zone\n    preheat_data = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_START_TEMP) &\n        (tc_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_END_TEMP)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n        return tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0], 0.0\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat_data['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat_data['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            dtemp = temps[i] - temps[i-1]\n            ramp_rate = dtemp / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(ramp_rate)\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates) if ramp_rates else 0.0\n    tc_id = preheat_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n    return tc_id, max_ramp\n\n\ndef process_question1(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q1: Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the most representative thermocouple (largest mass device as per handbook).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 1: Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_tc_data = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data for this run\n            continue\n\n        # According to handbook, use thermocouple on largest mass component\n        # The tc_location field indicates 'largest_mass' or 'smallest_mass'\n        largest_mass_data = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if len(largest_mass_data) > 0:\n            # Use largest mass thermocouple\n            tc_id, max_ramp = calculate_max_preheat_ramp_rate(largest_mass_data)\n        else:\n            # Fallback: use first available thermocouple\n            tc_groups = run_tc_data.groupby('tc_id')\n            tc_id = list(tc_groups.groups.keys())[0]\n            tc_id, max_ramp = calculate_max_preheat_ramp_rate(tc_groups.get_group(tc_id))\n\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"] = sorted(results[\"violating_runs\"])\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs\")\n    print(f\"  Violating runs: {len(results['violating_runs'])}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_tal(tc_data: pd.DataFrame, liquidus_temp: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus (TAL) in seconds.\n\n    Args:\n        tc_data: Thermocouple data for a single run and TC\n        liquidus_temp: Solder liquidus temperature in °C\n\n    Returns:\n        TAL in seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter data above liquidus temperature\n    above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Find continuous segments above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n\n    if len(times) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Simple approach: time from first to last point above liquidus\n    # More accurate: sum of time intervals\n    total_tal = 0.0\n\n    # Check for continuous segments\n    for i in range(len(times) - 1):\n        time_diff = times[i+1] - times[i]\n        # If consecutive points (assume 10s sampling interval)\n        if time_diff <= 20:  # Allow some tolerance\n            total_tal += time_diff\n\n    return total_tal\n\n\ndef process_question2(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q2: Calculate Time Above Liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_info = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n        liquidus_temp = run_info['solder_liquidus_c']\n\n        run_tc_data = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use largest mass thermocouple as per handbook\n        largest_mass_data = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if len(largest_mass_data) > 0:\n            tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n            tal = calculate_tal(largest_mass_data, liquidus_temp)\n        else:\n            # Fallback: use first available thermocouple\n            tc_groups = run_tc_data.groupby('tc_id')\n            tc_id = list(tc_groups.groups.keys())[0]\n            tc_group_data = tc_groups.get_group(tc_id)\n            tal = calculate_tal(tc_group_data, liquidus_temp)\n\n        # Determine compliance status\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results)} runs\")\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in results if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"  Compliant runs: {compliant}/{len(results)}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef get_peak_temperature(tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Get peak (maximum) temperature from thermocouple data.\"\"\"\n    if len(tc_data) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    return tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n\ndef process_question3(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q3: Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Requirement: Peak temp >= Liquidus + 20°C\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 3: Peak Temperature ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_info = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n        liquidus_temp = run_info['solder_liquidus_c']\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET\n\n        run_tc_data = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data - run is failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Get actual conveyor speed from MES log\n        actual_speed = run_info['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on board geometry\n        # From handbook: LINE SPEED (MIN) = BOARDS/MIN × LENGTH/BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        boards_per_min = run_info['boards_per_min']\n        board_length = run_info['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = run_info['loading_factor']\n\n        if loading_factor > 0:\n            required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length) / loading_factor\n        else:\n            required_min_speed = 0.0\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results)} runs\")\n    meeting = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"  Meeting requirements: {meeting}/{len(results)}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef process_question5(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, defects: pd.DataFrame, q1_results: Dict,\n                      q2_results: List[Dict], q3_results: Dict, q4_results: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q5: Select the best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Criteria for \"best\" run:\n    1. Highest yield (quality)\n    2. Profile compliance (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4)\n    3. Highest throughput (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 5: Best Run Selection ===\")\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q2_lookup = {r['run_id']: r['status'] for r in q2_results}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_lookup = {r['run_id']: r['meets'] for r in q4_results}\n    q1_violating = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n\n    # Get yield data from defects\n    yield_data = defects[defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    yield_dict = {}\n    for _, row in yield_data.iterrows():\n        if pd.notna(row['fp_yield_pct']):\n            yield_dict[row['run_id']] = row['fp_yield_pct']\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Group runs by board_family\n    board_families = sorted(mes_log['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        family_runs = mes_log[mes_log['board_family'] == board_family]\n\n        # Score each run\n        run_scores = []\n\n        for _, run_info in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_info['run_id']\n\n            # Quality score (0-100 from yield)\n            quality_score = yield_dict.get(run_id, 0.0)\n\n            # Compliance score (0 or 1 for each criterion)\n            compliance_score = 0\n            if run_id not in q1_violating:\n                compliance_score += 1\n            if q2_lookup.get(run_id) == 'compliant':\n                compliance_score += 1\n            if run_id not in q3_failing:\n                compliance_score += 1\n            if q4_lookup.get(run_id, False):\n                compliance_score += 1\n\n            # Efficiency score (boards per minute)\n            efficiency_score = run_info['boards_per_min']\n\n            # Combined score: prioritize compliance, then quality, then efficiency\n            combined_score = (compliance_score * 1000) + quality_score + (efficiency_score * 0.1)\n\n            run_scores.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': combined_score,\n                'quality': quality_score,\n                'compliance': compliance_score,\n                'efficiency': efficiency_score\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score (descending)\n        run_scores.sort(key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)\n\n        # Best run is the highest scoring\n        best_run = run_scores[0]['run_id']\n        runner_ups = [r['run_id'] for r in run_scores[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_ups\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results)} board families\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # °C\nPREHEAT_END_TEMP = 150.0    # °C\n\n# Maximum preheat ramp rate (Page 5)\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\n\n# Time Above Liquidus (TAL) requirements (Page 5)\nMIN_TAL = 30.0  # seconds\nMAX_TAL = 60.0  # seconds\n\n# Peak temperature requirement (Page 5)\nPEAK_TEMP_OFFSET = 20.0  # °C above liquidus\n\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    print(f\"  MES log: {len(mes_log)} runs\")\n    print(f\"  Thermocouple data: {len(thermocouples)} records\")\n    print(f\"  Defect data: {len(defects)} records\")\n\n    return mes_log, thermocouples, defects\n\n\ndef calculate_max_preheat_ramp_rate(tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> Tuple[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate maximum ramp rate during preheat zone (100-150°C) for a single TC.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (tc_id, max_ramp_rate_c_per_s)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter data in preheat zone\n    preheat_data = tc_data[\n        (tc_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_START_TEMP) &\n        (tc_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_END_TEMP)\n    ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n        return tc_data['tc_id'].iloc[0], 0.0\n\n    # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n    temps = preheat_data['temp_c'].values\n    times = preheat_data['time_s'].values\n\n    ramp_rates = []\n    for i in range(1, len(temps)):\n        dt = times[i] - times[i-1]\n        if dt > 0:\n            dtemp = temps[i] - temps[i-1]\n            ramp_rate = dtemp / dt\n            ramp_rates.append(ramp_rate)\n\n    max_ramp = max(ramp_rates) if ramp_rates else 0.0\n    tc_id = preheat_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n    return tc_id, max_ramp\n\n\ndef process_question1(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q1: Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n    Use the most representative thermocouple (largest mass device as per handbook).\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 1: Preheat Ramp Rate ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_tc_data = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data for this run\n            continue\n\n        # According to handbook, use thermocouple on largest mass component\n        # The tc_location field indicates 'largest_mass' or 'smallest_mass'\n        largest_mass_data = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if len(largest_mass_data) > 0:\n            # Use largest mass thermocouple\n            tc_id, max_ramp = calculate_max_preheat_ramp_rate(largest_mass_data)\n        else:\n            # Fallback: use first available thermocouple\n            tc_groups = run_tc_data.groupby('tc_id')\n            tc_id = list(tc_groups.groups.keys())[0]\n            tc_id, max_ramp = calculate_max_preheat_ramp_rate(tc_groups.get_group(tc_id))\n\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"] = sorted(results[\"violating_runs\"])\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results['max_ramp_by_run'])} runs\")\n    print(f\"  Violating runs: {len(results['violating_runs'])}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_tal(tc_data: pd.DataFrame, liquidus_temp: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Time Above Liquidus (TAL) in seconds.\n\n    Args:\n        tc_data: Thermocouple data for a single run and TC\n        liquidus_temp: Solder liquidus temperature in °C\n\n    Returns:\n        TAL in seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter data above liquidus temperature\n    above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].sort_values('time_s')\n\n    if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Find continuous segments above liquidus\n    times = above_liquidus['time_s'].values\n\n    if len(times) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Simple approach: time from first to last point above liquidus\n    # More accurate: sum of time intervals\n    total_tal = 0.0\n\n    # Check for continuous segments\n    for i in range(len(times) - 1):\n        time_diff = times[i+1] - times[i]\n        # If consecutive points (assume 10s sampling interval)\n        if time_diff <= 20:  # Allow some tolerance\n            total_tal += time_diff\n\n    return total_tal\n\n\ndef process_question2(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q2: Calculate Time Above Liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_info = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n        liquidus_temp = run_info['solder_liquidus_c']\n\n        run_tc_data = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Use largest mass thermocouple as per handbook\n        largest_mass_data = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if len(largest_mass_data) > 0:\n            tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n            tal = calculate_tal(largest_mass_data, liquidus_temp)\n        else:\n            # Fallback: use first available thermocouple\n            tc_groups = run_tc_data.groupby('tc_id')\n            tc_id = list(tc_groups.groups.keys())[0]\n            tc_group_data = tc_groups.get_group(tc_id)\n            tal = calculate_tal(tc_group_data, liquidus_temp)\n\n        # Determine compliance status\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL <= tal <= MAX_TAL) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL,\n            \"status\": status\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results)} runs\")\n    compliant = sum(1 for r in results if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"  Compliant runs: {compliant}/{len(results)}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef get_peak_temperature(tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Get peak (maximum) temperature from thermocouple data.\"\"\"\n    if len(tc_data) == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    return tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n\ndef process_question3(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, tc_data: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q3: Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n    Requirement: Peak temp >= Liquidus + 20°C\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 3: Peak Temperature ===\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_info = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n        liquidus_temp = run_info['solder_liquidus_c']\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET\n\n        run_tc_data = tc_data[tc_data['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if len(run_tc_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data - run is failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Use largest mass thermocouple (worst case - coldest spot)\n        largest_mass_data = run_tc_data[run_tc_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if len(largest_mass_data) > 0:\n            tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n            peak_temp = get_peak_temperature(largest_mass_data)\n        else:\n            # Check if failing\n        if peak_temp < required_min_peak:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    results[\"failing_runs\"] = sorted(results[\"failing_runs\"])\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results['min_peak_by_run'])} runs\")\n    print(f\"  Failing runs: {len(results['failing_runs'])}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef process_question4(mes_log: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q4: Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n\n    Based on handbook Page 14:\n    - Minimum line speed is based on board geometry and throughput\n    - The actual conveyor speed should be checked against thermal requirements\n\n    For this analysis, we'll use a simplified approach based on the data available.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility ===\")\n\n    results = []\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_info = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get actual conveyor speed from MES log\n        actual_speed = run_info['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate required minimum speed based on board geometry\n        # From handbook: LINE SPEED (MIN) = BOARDS/MIN × LENGTH/BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        boards_per_min = run_info['boards_per_min']\n        board_length = run_info['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = run_info['loading_factor']\n\n        if loading_factor > 0:\n            required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length) / loading_factor\n        else:\n            required_min_speed = 0.0\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results)} runs\")\n    meeting = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"  Meeting requirements: {meeting}/{len(results)}\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef process_question5(mes_log: pd.DataFrame, defects: pd.DataFrame, q1_results: Dict,\n                      q2_results: List[Dict], q3_results: Dict, q4_results: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Q5: Select the best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Criteria for \"best\" run:\n    1. Highest yield (quality)\n    2. Profile compliance (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4)\n    3. Highest throughput (efficiency)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Question 5: Best Run Selection ===\")\n\n    # Create compliance lookup dictionaries\n    q2_lookup = {r['run_id']: r['status'] for r in q2_results}\n    q3_failing = set(q3_results['failing_runs'])\n    q4_lookup = {r['run_id']: r['meets'] for r in q4_results}\n    q1_violating = set(q1_results['violating_runs'])\n\n    # Get yield data from defects\n    yield_data = defects[defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'].copy()\n    yield_dict = {}\n    for _, row in yield_data.iterrows():\n        if pd.notna(row['fp_yield_pct']):\n            yield_dict[row['run_id']] = row['fp_yield_pct']\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Group runs by board_family\n    board_families = sorted(mes_log['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        family_runs = mes_log[mes_log['board_family'] == board_family]\n\n        # Score each run\n        run_scores = []\n\n        for _, run_info in family_runs.iterrows():\n            run_id = run_info['run_id']\n\n            # Quality score (0-100 from yield)\n            quality_score = yield_dict.get(run_id, 0.0)\n\n            # Compliance score (0 or 1 for each criterion)\n            compliance_score = 0\n            if run_id not in q1_violating:\n                compliance_score += 1\n            if q2_lookup.get(run_id) == 'compliant':\n                compliance_score += 1\n            if run_id not in q3_failing:\n                compliance_score += 1\n            if q4_lookup.get(run_id, False):\n                compliance_score += 1\n\n            # Efficiency score (boards per minute)\n            efficiency_score = run_info['boards_per_min']\n\n            # Combined score: prioritize compliance, then quality, then efficiency\n            combined_score = (compliance_score * 1000) + quality_score + (efficiency_score * 0.1)\n\n            run_scores.append({\n                'run_id': run_id,\n                'score': combined_score,\n                'quality': quality_score,\n                'compliance': compliance_score,\n                'efficiency': efficiency_score\n            })\n\n        # Sort by score (descending)\n        run_scores.sort(key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)\n\n        # Best run is the highest scoring\n        best_run = run_scores[0]['run_id']\n        runner_ups = [r['run_id'] for r in run_scores[1:]]\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_ups\n        })\n\n    print(f\"  Analyzed {len(results)} board families\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    #
